    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [g].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]

    WHERE [g1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [g2] ON [s].[Id0] = [g2].[GymId]
